金融市場投資週報                 2026 年 9 月 21 日
上週重點訊息
◼ Fed 三年來首次升息，點陣圖顯示年底前再升一碼：Fed 於 9/16 一致決議升息 1 碼，將聯
邦基金利率目標區間提高至 3.75%～4.00%，為 2023 年 7 月以來首次升息。最新利率預測
中，18 名官員有 16 人預期年底前至少仍需再升息 1 碼。最新經濟預測顯示，Fed 上修今明
兩年實質 GDP 成長率並下修失業率預估值，但也預期通膨壓力將比原先估計更為頑強。主席
Warsh 並指出美國經濟、就業、內需與資本投資仍具韌性，金融環境難稱緊縮。

◼ AI 安全疑慮引發半導體股急跌，但科技產業內部仍呈現明顯分化： OpenAI、Anthropic 等
AI 業者高層呼籲放慢 AI 發展速度、強化安全防護，引發市場重新評估 AI 投資成長速度與資
本支出前景，拖累費城半導體指數 9/14 重挫約 5.9%，但軟體股反而上漲。現階段尚不足以
證明 AI 資本支出循環已反轉，資金亦未全面撤離科技產業，而是從估值較高、籌碼較擁擠的
AI 硬體轉向其他科技次產業。
◼ 日本央行決議升息 1 碼，符合市場預期：日本央行 9/18 以 7 比 2 決議將無擔保隔夜拆款利
率由 1.0%調升至 1.25%。此為該行 2024 年來第 6 次升息，符合市場預期，故升息後對風險
資產衝擊有限。BOJ 明言將「繼續升息並調整寬鬆程度」 ，並將審視調整的時點與步調。
市場展望與操作策略
◼ 科技股中期趨勢未見反轉，但短線波動料加劇：儘管 FOMC 9 月升息，點陣圖暗示今年可能
再升一碼，但其對經濟成長率與失業率預期仍朝正向發展，同時股市也止跌上揚，顯示投資

人認為企業獲利成長動能足以抵消利率上揚的負面影響，基本面較佳的科技股仍具資金承
接，惟宜留意股市短線仍可能因地緣政治與經濟數據表現而出現波動。
◼ 中天期美債及投資級債風險報酬相對均衡：Fed 重啟升息且點陣圖暗示年底前或再升息，短天
期公債殖利率直接受到政策利率上修影響 ； 天期過長的債券則持續面臨財政赤字 、 發債供給及
期限溢價上升壓力，利率敏感度偏高。中天期(5~10 年期)美國公債既可取得目前相對較高收
益，亦具適度存續期間，在未來通膨降溫時較有機會受惠於殖利率回落，風險報酬較為均衡。
投資級債亦具較高收益與信用防禦特性，有助平衡投資組合波動。

HTC Corp. (2498.TW): XR headset and AI glasses to support growth 
ahead; Near-term cost pressures remain; Sell
10 October 2026 | 12:24AM HKT
In September, HTC accelerated its global wearable expansion with the commercial 
launch of the new round-frame edition of its VIVE Eagle AI smart glasses alongside 
substantial software enhancements across North America, Europe, and Australia 
(news). Features include hands-free image capture, first-person POV live streaming, 
real-time AI translation, AI Notes, dashcam utility, and assistive vision for the visually 
impaired. Meanwhile, the company continues to anchor its VR headset strategy 
around commercial enterprise solutions, underscored by its flagship deployment 
with Axon which shifts hazardous tactical training into virtual environments to 
reduce operational costs, complemented by use cases in complex surgical 
simulation, interior space visualization, and virtual meeting rooms. 
While we maintain our constructive view on HTC’s long-term growth trajectory 
backed by sustained product innovation, we are Sell rated given near-term earnings 
headwinds - specifically core monetization drivers across XR/ VR/ AI glasses and 
software will require extended lead time to ramp adoption, compounded by 
consumer electronics demand softness and margin compression from elevated 
memory component costs. We would turn more positive if we see 
stronger-than-expected XR headset/ AI glasses growth driven by market demand, 
application development or customer expansion.  
Earnings revisions: We factor in 3Q26 revenues and revise down 2026-28E net 
income. We revise down 2026-28E revenues by 4%/ 0%/ 27% due to demand 
pressures of consumer electronics (impacted by soaring memory cost) and soft 
demand of XR products in the near-term. GM estimates are largely unchanged in 
2026-2027E, and we revise down our OPM estimates by 2.1/ 0.3ppts on a smaller 
business scale. GM are revised up by 5.7ppts in 2028E to reflect mix upgrade to VR 
hardware and software with high margin.

### Page 2

Valuation: With earnings revisions and an unchanged valuation methodology, our 12M 
target price is revised down to NT$32.8 (vs. previously at NT$34.4). We continue to 
derive our target price via 85% based on a fundamental valuation and 15% from a 
theoretical M&A valuation. (1) Our fundamental valuation is based on 2028E P/E of 
19.3x (unchanged), discounted back to 2026E at an 11.5% COE. (2) Our M&A valuation is 
based on 23.6x 2028E P/E (unchanged) then discounted back to 2026E at an 11.5% 
COE. 
Our target 2028E discounted P/E multiple of 19.3x is in line with XR peers’ historical avg. 
P/E at 19.3x in 2022 (method unchanged, Exhibit 5). HTC’s 2029 NI growth will be 15% 
YoY per our estimate, which is lower than XR peers’ (Meta/ Goertek/ Microsoft/ Apple/ 
Sony) 2023-24 avg. NI growth at 23% YoY. Our M&A target multiple is 23.6x 2028E P/E, 
which is derived from the peers’ historical avg. +1std P/E in 2022 (methodology 
unchanged). We use avg. +1std P/E of peers to reflect the M&A premium.  
Our 12M target price of NT$32.8 implies 4.1x 2026E P/S vs. 126% revenue YoY growth 
on average in 2026E-27E. The implied 2026E P/S of 4.1x is lower than the company’s 
current price implied 2026E P/S at 4.9x, showing that valuation is stretched. Maintain 
Sell.

### Page 3

Price Target Risks and Methodology - HTC Corp. 
Valuation methodology: We are Sell rated on HTC with a 12M target price of NT$32.8, 
which is derived 85% from a fundamental valuation and 15% from a theoretical M&A 
valuation. (1) Our fundamental valuation is based on 19.3x 2028E P/E, discounted back 
to 2026E at an 11.5% COE. The 19.3x target P/E multiple is derived from peers’ avg. P/E 
in 2022; (2) Our M&A valuation is based on 23.6x 2028E P/E and discounted back to 
2026E at an 11.5% COE. 
Key risks: 1) Better-than-expected VR demand; 2) lower-than-expected competition in 
VR headsets; and 3) lower-than-expected price pressure from peers.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.
Not Rated (NR). The investment rating, target price and earnings estimates (where relevant) are removed pursuant to Goldman Sachs policy when
Goldman Sachs is acting in an advisory capacity in a merger or in a strategic transaction involving this company, when there are legal, regulatory or
policy constraints due to Goldman Sachs’ involvement in a transaction, and in certain other circumstances. Early-Stage Biotech (ES). An investment

rating and a target price are not assigned pursuant to Goldman Sachs policy when this company has neither a drug, treatment or medical device that
has passed a Phase II clinical trial nor a license to distribute a post-Phase II drug, treatment or medical device.  Rating Suspended (RS). Goldman Sachs
Research has suspended the investment rating and price target for this stock, because there is not a sufficient fundamental basis for determining an
investment rating or target price. The previous investment rating and target price, if any, are no longer in effect for this stock and should not be relied

upon.  Coverage Suspended (CS). Goldman Sachs has suspended coverage of this company.  Not Covered (NC). Goldman Sachs does not cover this
company.

AirTAC International  | Asia Pacific
September Sales Rose 3%
MoM, 27% YoY
Here we focus on Airtac's monthly sales, which we believe could
be a catalyst for its share price.
What's new:
• September sales came in at NT$3,811mn (+3% MoM/+27% YoY).
• 3Q26 revenue was NT$11,512mn (-5% QoQ/+40% YoY), in line with our
estimate of NT$11,586mn (-5% QoQ/+41% YoY) but slightly better than
consensus at NT$11,215mn (-8% QoQ/+36% YoY).
• By segment, shipments of machine tool (+35% YoY), energy & lighting (+30%

YoY), electronics (+27% YoY) and textiles (+25% YoY) outperformed during
the month.
Outlook:
• Both order booking and shipments continued to exceed company
expectations in the month.
• Looking ahead, management remains positive on its growth outlook, driven
by share gains and stimulus policies, which could help sustain the upcycle at
least into 2027.
• We remain OW as current valuation at 18x 2027e P/E is undemanding vs. its
mid-cycle average of 25x, though we think Hiwin (2049.TW) might

outperform on share price on the back of sequential growth in the top line
and potentially GM in 3Q26.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
AirTAC International (1590.TW)
Base case, 29x 2027e P/E. We believe this methodology better captures earnings growth
momentum for industrial automation component plays (market sentiment moves along
with business cycles). As the industry enters an up-cycle, we think applying a near-peak cycle
valuation of 29x P/E is reasonable given the stronger business outlook.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger macroeconomic climate in China

n Stronger-than-expected demand from industrial automation
Risks to Downside
n Sharper economic downturn in China
n Miniature linear guideway business development taking more time
n Larger-than-expected investment in new products but weaker-than-expected demand
Hiwin Technologies Corp. (2049.TW)
Base case, P/E. We think this valuation methodology better captures Hiwin's earnings growth
momentum, as its valuation tends to move with the business cycle. We apply a target multiple

of 38x to our 2027 EPS estimate. We view this multiple as justified by the 47% operating profit
CAGR we project for 2025-28.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger industrial automation demand pickup.
n Market share gains.
n Earlier-than-expected revenue contribution from humanoids.
Risks to Downside
n Weaker industrial automation demand.
n More pricing pressure.
n Market share loss.
n More broad-based impact on the global economy from geopolitical risks.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

New capacity announcements do not overturn our bullish view.
AI demand and increasing substrate complexity should keep the
market undersupplied from 2027, supporting further pricing and
margin expansion. We raise earnings estimates and price targets
for Unimicron and NYPCB and reiterate OW on both.
More capacity, but limited near-term relief: Suppliers are stepping up investment,

but several newly announced projects are not scheduled to ramp until late 2028-30
or beyond. Our updated supply-demand model continues to indicate undersupply
from 2027 through 2030, with tighter supply in CY27-28, which should only be
more favorable to pricing. Therefore, capacity additions announced in the past few
months do not, in our view, diminish the magnitude of pricing gains over the next
two years.
CY27 pricing should accelerate, amid tighter supply-demand in CY27-28, but

visibility on the magnitude remains limited: We see ABF substrate prices rising
25%+ in CY27, below buy-side expectations of ~50%. Our supply/demand outlook
points to undersupply next year, with larger substrates, higher layer counts, and
greater manufacturing complexity further constraining usable output. Raw material
cost pass-through was a key driver of CY26 price increases, we expect supply
tightness to play a greater role in CY27. This should allow pricing to outpace cost

inflation, driving faster margin expansion. We remain constructive on pricing, but the
magnitude and timing of increases depends on customer-level negotiations.
With pricing a segment-wide tailwind, we prefer Unimicron for its stronger CY27
capacity growth: As supply tightens, the ability to deliver incremental volume
should become a key differentiator. We favor suppliers bringing capacity online in
CY27 over those whose expansion plans contribute in later years. Unimicron’s

Guangfu II ramp should add 10-15% to capacity in CY27, compared with 5-10%
growth at NYPCB through debottlenecking, giving Unimicron greater scope to
benefit from both pricing and volume growth. Unimicron is also our preferred play
heading into earnings, as more demanding expectations for NYPCB leave less room
for an upside surprise. But we remain OW on NYPCB given its networking and AMD
CPU exposure, alongside its proactive pricing strategy.
We reiterate Overweight on Unimicron and NYPCB: Reflecting stronger earnings

trajectories, we raise our EPS estimates for both companies. Based on upside to our
revised price targets, our order of preference is Unimicron (NT$1,775 PT; 24x CY28e
P/E; 41% upside), followed by NYPCB (NT$2,000 PT; 22x CY28e P/E; 39% upside).
Potential risks: 1) Weaker demand for PCs, general servers, and AI servers; 2)
meaningful capacity expansion plans; 3) T-glass shortage; 4) CoWoP displacing ABF
substrates.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.

### Page 2

M
Idea
2
Supply/Demand Update: Undersupply Deepens in CY27–28
Our updated model points to a wider supply shortfall in CY27–28, despite additional
capacity: Incorporating the latest expansion plans and semiconductor demand forecasts,
we now expect ABF substrate demand to exceed available supply by 9.3% in CY27 and
13.7% in CY28, versus 6.2% and 9.4% previously. This represents a widening of 3.1ppt and
4.3ppt, respectively, relative to our prior estimates. We maintain our view that CY26 is a

broadly balanced market, followed by increasingly tight supply over the next two years.
The main demand upgrade comes from other ASICs, including Chinese AI chips: We
raise our shipment assumptions for this category to 6.4mn units in CY27 and 8.7mn in
CY28, from 4.6mn and 6.2mn previously. Our Nvidia GPU and server CPU forecasts remain
largely unchanged, while we lower AMD GPU estimates while also keeping CY27 Trainium
and TPU shipments largely unchanged. The tighter substrate outlook therefore reflects

stronger demand across a broader set of ASIC suppliers, rather than higher shipment
assumptions for every major AI platform. We also raise our substrate content assumptions
for select chips based on our latest supply chain checks, lifting our substrate demand
estimates across some segments, including server CPUs and networking.
Higher supply assumptions provide limited relief in CY27-28: We now include additional
capacity announcements that have been announced recently, including two additional

Kinsus factories, scheduled to come online in CY28 and CY29, beyond the CY27 expansion
already in our model. Several larger projects, however, contribute later. NYPCB’s new site
starts ramping in 2H29, while SEMCO’s Sejong expansion begins mass production in
September 2028 and its project in Vietnam is scheduled to begin operations by June
2030. These additions increase medium-term supply but do not prevent the modeled
shortfall from worsening through CY28.

Increasing substrate complexity adds to the pressure on usable output: Larger body
sizes, higher layer counts, and more demanding manufacturing processes constrain the
output available to meet demand, even as suppliers expand installed capacity. With the
supply gap widening in CY27-28, we expect tightness to become a more important pricing
driver than raw material cost pass-through. This supports our forecast for 25%+ price
increases in CY27 and further increases in CY28, alongside continued margin expansion.

Supply-side developments
Unimicron (3037.TW, OW, covered by Howard Kao)
On September 30, Unimicron announced that it has acquired a Hukou site from
Bridgestone Taiwan for ~NT$10.089bn (~US$318mn). Based on our checks, this is for
capacity expansion plans in 2030 or beyond.
For CY26, Unimicron has announced capex of NT$53.7B.

Over the next few years, Unimicron already has several new production facilities coming
online, including:
•  Guangfu II: ramp in 2027
• Yangmei II: ramp in 2028
• Yangmei III: ramp in 2029
• Yangmei IV: ramp in 2029+
• Bridgestone: ramp in 2030+

### Page 4

M
Idea
4
NYPCB (8046.TW, OW, covered by Howard Kao)
On August 6, NYPCB announced that its Board of Directors had approved a capex budget
of NT$46.8bn (~US$1,474mn) to invest in building a new ABF substrate production site.
Our checks indicate that this production site is located in the southern part of Taiwan, and
will start to ramp up production in 2H29. Given the size of this investment, we estimate
that it will approximately double its capacity exiting CY30, relative to its CY28 capacity.

Kinsus (3189.TW, Not Covered)
In our previous model, we had assumed only one factory (k6b) coming online in CY27,
increasing capacity ~30%. However, recent checks suggest that Kinsus intends to bring
two more factories online, one in CY28 (k6c) and one in CY29 (k6d). This implies that
capacity would increase another ~25% in CY28 and ~20% in CY29, bringing Kinsus' total
ABF substrate capacity to almost double exiting CY29, relative to its CY26 capacity.

Ibiden (4062.T, covered by Shoji Sato)
Back in February, Ibiden first announced that it will invest capex of ¥500bn in the
electronics field planned through F3/29, for ABF substrate expansion. Sato-san's latest
view is that Ibiden will need a new factory building to install the equipment and facilities
that will be purchased with the ¥500bn. As a result, Sato-san expects Ibiden during its 2Q
results briefing (October 30) to announce plans to make a large capital investment,

including for a factory building with a long lead time, and raise its medium-term earnings
forecast (report here).
SEMCO (009150.KS, covered by Shawn Kim)
On September 29, SEMCO announced investment of KRW4.27 trillion (~US$3,178mn) in
Sejong and KRW2.51 trillion (~US$1,488mn) in Vietnam, for AI server semiconductor
substrate capacity expansion.
• Sejong: Investment period from September 2026 to May 2028, with mass
production starting in September 2028. Largest single-product investment to date,

serving as key hub for mass production of high-value-added, highly demanding
products, proactively responding to the increasing demand for AI server substrates.
• Vietnam: The Vietnamese subsidiary aims to complete construction and begin
operations by June 2030. It will be operated as a strategic hub to respond to global
volume demand and enhance production efficiency through a stable supply
system.
With this investment, Samsung Electro-Mechanics plans to actively respond to the

growing demand for high-value-added semiconductor substrates in high-growth markets
such as AI servers, HPC, and automotive electronics. In particular, this investment is being
pursued based on medium- to long-term cooperation with global big tech and
semiconductor customers. The investment includes customer funding support and
medium- to long-term volume guarantees; thus, the company expects to strengthen the
competitiveness of its FCBGA business based on a stable demand foundation.

LG Innotek (011070.KS, covered by Ryan Kim)

### Page 5

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 5
According to news reports (link), LG Innotek signed a memorandum of understanding
(MOU) with Haiphong, Vietnam, for an investment to expand its semiconductor substrate
plant. Under the agreement, LG Innotek will push to build a semiconductor substrate
plant in Haiphong.
•  Construction will begin in July 2026 and is scheduled to be completed in May
2027.
• The site spans approximately 330,000 square meters (98,000 pyeong), equivalent

to 45 soccer fields.
• According to a news report, LG Innotek will invest approximately $1 billion, or
about 1.53 trillion won, for its Phase 1 expansion plan, which mainly includes RF-SiP
(radio frequency system-in-package) and FC-CSP (flip chip-chip scale package).
• ABF substrates will be part of the Phase 2 expansion plan, but no details or
investment size has been disclosed, yet.
According to a press release from LG Innotek on September 30, LG Innotek started mass

production of ABF substrates for PC chipsets targeting big tech clients last year. It
initiated high-volume mass production of FC-BGA for PC CPUs in the third quarter of this
year. Simultaneously, the company is rapidly boosting revenue by securing additional
major clients. Starting next year, it plans to sequentially enter the AI accelerator and AI
server markets.
• Revenue from LG Innotek's package solution business has maintained an upward
trajectory for three consecutive years. With the package solution business

demonstrating tangible results alongside its optical solution business, the
company has strengthened its capacity to generate stable earnings.
• LG Innotek plans to rapidly expand its market share in the emerging semiconductor
substrate market, aiming to build its package solution business, including
semiconductor substrates, into a business generating US$743 million in operating
profit by 2031.
• In the future, LG Innotek plans to develop its future businesses, including

semiconductor substrates and physical AI solutions, to US$5.94 billion by 2030.
The company aims to have such future new businesses account for 25% of total
revenue, thereby completing a well-balanced business portfolio.
The expanded plant will produce major semiconductor substrates including radio
frequency system-in-package (RF-SiP), flip chip chip scale package (FC-CSP), and flip chip
ball grid array (FC-BGA). "The Gumi site will serve as a 'mother factory' responsible for

developing new semiconductor substrate technologies and producing new models and
high-value-added products, while the expanded plant in Vietnam will be operated as a
production base for general-purpose semiconductor substrates," an LG Innotek official
explained.
AT&S (ATSV.VI, Not Covered)
On June 15, AT&S announced that it had signed key terms with AMD and Marvell to add
AI/HPC IC substrate capacity in Kulim, Malaysia. The company said the €1.5-2bn

investment (approximately US$1.68bn to US$2.24bn) is fully supported and financed by
long-term customer commitments, pending final execution.

Shinko (private)
On September 30, Shinko announced that it has entered into a purchase agreement with
Japan Display Inc. (JDI) to acquire its Mobara fab. In addition, it has entered into a sale
agreement with FICT Ltd. for the transfer of a portion of the fab to be acquired.
• Shinko will position the fab as a new core plant for advanced semiconductor
packages, and strengthen its production systems for flip-chip type packages
through the determination and implementation of capital investment plans for the

fab in conjunction with capacity expansion at its plants in the Nagano area.
Through these initiatives, Shinko aims to respond accurately to strong market
demand while pursuing sustainable business growth and enhancing corporate
value.
• The JDI Mobara fab has a total land area of 373,483 square meters, with Shinko
using the eastern area of the Fab (140,738 square meters, or ~38%) and FICT
planning to use the western area (232,745 square meters, or ~62%).

Toppan (7911.T, Not Covered)
According to news, Toppan broke ground in March 2024 on a new factory on Pesawat
Drive near Singapore's Jurong Lake District, built through its local unit Advanced Substrate
Technologies (AST), a JV with Broadcom. It makes FC-BGA substrates, which is Toppan's
term for them. It is Toppan's first FC-BGA plant outside Japan. The plant opening
ceremony took place on September 30, 2026.
• AST operates a facility of approximately 70,000 m² in the vicinity of Jurong Lake

District and employs more than 200 highly skilled engineers and technicians.
• Focus on high-demand, large-size, high-layer-count substrates
• While the investment size is not disclosed directly by the company, Nikkei sized
the investment at around ¥50 billion (~US$317mn).
Daeduck (353200.KS, Not Covered)
Daeduck announced on July 20, 2026, that it is investing ₩497 billion (~US$370M)
manufacturing facilities and related infrastructure for semiconductor products, running

through December 31, 2027.
ABF capex is stepping up, but announced investment still falls short of implied
demand. We estimate that ABF substrate makers have announced ~US$14.3bn of
investment year to date, materially above the segment’s aggregate annual capex in recent
years, although these commitments span multiple years. Our tally is not exhaustive. LG
Innotek has yet to disclose the size of its planned ABF capacity investment, while Shinko
has not quantified the additional spending required to expand capacity following its

announced acquisition of a JDI fab.
Our supply chain checks suggest that each US$1 of investment can support US$0.50–
0.60 of annual revenue at full ramp. On this basis, the US$14.3bn announced so far could
support US$7.2–8.6bn of incremental annual revenue. This compares with US$11.4bn of
incremental revenue implied by our industry forecast. We model the ABF substrate
market reaching US$20.9bn in 2030, up from US$9.5bn in 2026. Despite the step-up in

meet our demand forecast, pointing to a need for further capacity expansion over the
coming years.

The State of the Current Cycle
The ABF substrate market has gone through a few cycles over the past two
decades. We believe we are now still in the early innings of an up-cycle.
There was a prolonged down-cycle from 2012-18 as the PC TAM shrank amid the rise of
smartphones and tablets. During this tough period, companies were not able to generate
profits and had to compete on price to fill their idle capacity. As a result, during those

years, there was no or very limited capacity expansion, and capital expenditures by the
major ABF substrate suppliers were kept at a minimum.
Even as general server demand picked up, lifting utilization rates across the group going
into 2020, ABF substrate suppliers remained cautious on the longer-term demand outlook
and remained disciplined, hesitating to expand capacity. Hence, capex turned up just
slightly in 2019-20, increasing ~US$600mn vs. ~US$2.2bn on average per year in 2012-18.

This underinvestment led to sudden undersupply, as the shock from Covid-19 drove strong
work-from-home demand for PCs, as well as increased data usage and storage for general
computing driven by online entertainment. This ultimately led to a significant increase in
ABF substrate market value from 2020-22, reflected by the sudden surge in demand
along with meaningful pricing adjustments owing to the lack of supply.
During this 2020-22 period, much capacity expansion was set in motion as ABF substrate

suppliers and customers came to agree that necessary investments were needed for
future growth. Some subsidies were even given to these suppliers to facilitate this
capacity expansion. This is why capex jumped from ~US$2.9bn in 2020 to US$4.1bn in
2021 and US$5.5bn in 2022; even in 2023-24, capex remained elevated, as capacity
expansion plans continued.
Unfortunately, demand did not play out as expected. As the world came out of the
pandemic, demand for PCs and online entertainment fell sharply. This normalization of

demand, coupled with capacity expansion from practically every major ABF substrate
supplier, resulted in the ABF substrate market once again becoming oversupplied. This led
to a sharp decline in ABF substrate market value in 2023-24, as volumes normalized,
driving prices down to normalize against the new supply/demand dynamic.
Fortunately, cloud demand remained strong for general computing, and with AI demand
starting to pick up, the down-cycle this time around was much shorter (two years) vs. the

prior down-cycle (six years).
In 1H24, the ABF substrate market troughed and we saw an upward inflection since then,
but the pace of the recovery has been moderate and bifurcated. Midrange/low-end
markets such as PCs have shown moderate demand recovery, with high-end server and AI-
related end-demand recovering and growing at a faster pace. This is why we turned the
page on our bearish stance in 1H25, but did not turn more bullish until early 2026.

### Page 14

M
Idea
14
Periods Description
Period I (2010-2012): PC-centric
equilibrium
ABF substrate demand is primarily driven and dominated by CPU and GPU demand for desktops, notebooks,
discrete GPUs, with a meaningfully smaller exposure towards server CPUs.
Period II (2012-16): PC decline
and demand dilution
Global PC demand entered  a structural down-cycle as consumer behavior changed, with smartphones and tablets
absorbing more consumer time spent. This resulted in lower volumes and increased pricing competition as

suppliers fought to fill idle capacity.
Period III (2016-18): The quiet
inflection - servers and early
HPC
During these years, the PC market matured, while cloud computing started to take off. At the same time, server
CPU substrates quietly grew larger with more layers. We also started to see early accelerators being adopted in
high performance computing.
Period IV (2018-2022): WFH
demand driven structural
shortage
The Covid-19 pandemic drove massive work from home demand for consumer electronics and cloud computing.

This sudden surge in PC demand and increased time spent on gaming and online entertainment, bundled with a
continuous increase in substrate package size and layer count, resulted in a structural shortage, which also resulted
in longer lead times and raised prices. It was during this period (2021-22) that ABF suppliers globally started to
invest in new capacity.
Period V (2022-2024): Post-
pandemic correction and new
capacity arrival
As the world came out of the Covid-19 pandemic, WFH demand faded and  PC and cloud computing demand were

negatively affected. But at the same time, the new investments and capacity additions were just starting to slowly
come online, resulting in a drop in utilization rates and pricing.
Period VI (2024-2026):
Bifurcated recovery
PC demand normalized at the pre-Covid levels, with some replacement demand driven by Windows upgrade, while
the recovery and growth of AI and general server demand for cloud picks up at a meaningfully higher growth
trajectory.

Period VII (2026-30): AI diffusion Demand for AI proliferates from training to inference, driving ABF substrate market growth to re-accelerate over the
next five years after a two-year down-cycle following the Covid-19 pandemic. This drives increased demand for AI
computing, networking, and cloud computing, while demand for PCs remains soft.

Breaking down ABF substrate end demand
The end market mix for ABF substrates has shifted dramatically over the past decade. PCs

were once the dominant demand driver, accounting for approximately 70% of global ABF
substrate market value in 2015, supported primarily by PC CPUs and GPUs. Since then,
however, the industry's demand profile has undergone a structural transformation. We
estimate that PC-related applications represented only ~23% of total ABF substrate
market value in 2025, and this share is expected to decline further to below 10% by 2030.
In contrast, demand from servers, AI GPUs, AI ASICs, and networking applications has

expanded rapidly. We estimate that these segments collectively accounted for only
around 10% of the ABF substrate market in 2015, but their share increased to ~60% by
2025. Looking ahead, we expect they will represent more than 80% of total ABF
substrate market value by 2030.
In our view, this structural shift in end-market demand has been the primary driver of ABF
substrate industry growth over the past decade and will become even more pronounced

over the next five years. As AI infrastructure deployment accelerates, we expect continued
expansion in server, AI accelerator, and networking demand to drive meaningful
acceleration in ABF substrate market growth.

Price Target and Valuation Methodology
Our 12-month price target increases ~21% to NT$1,775 (from NT$1,465), driven by our
increased earnings estimates for CY26-28: We believe the company's earnings have
returned to a growth track from 2026 onward and will show stronger  growth in 2027,
driven by the high-end ABF substrate market's reversion to undersupply and Unimicron's
dominant supply share in AI ASICs and also server CPUs, which puts it in a favorable

position to benefit from rising AI investments.
We believe the overall ABF substrate market bottomed in 2H24, and see growth
accelerating from 2H26 onward with the ramp of multiple next-generation, large body size
ABF substrates for new server GPUs, CPUs, ASICs, and networking chips. New
technologies such as embedded passives and Intel's EMIB-T should also increase the
manufacturing complexity of the package substrates, which adds to the under-capacity as

usable output decreases.
Similar to our analysis of other tech hardware companies within our coverage, we use a
residual income (RI) valuation model to value Unimicron – we think it derives the most
accurate value of the firm as it takes into account cost of equity. Our key assumptions
include:
• Cost of equity of 9.2% (unchanged)
° Risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield);
° Equity risk premium of 8.7%;
° Beta of 1.0;
• Medium-term growth rate of 14% (slightly lowered from 15%)

• Terminal growth rate of 3% (unchanged, similar to the rest of the companies under
our tech hardware coverage).
Our bull and bear case values increase by similar magnitudes, to NT$3,030 and NT
$595, respectively.

Price Target and Valuation Methodology
Our price target increases ~29%, to NT$2,000, reflecting our raised earnings
estimates for CY26-28: We believe the company's earnings have returned to a growth
track from 2026 onward and will show stronger growth in 2027, driven by the high-end
ABF substrate market's reversion to undersupply, NYPCB's dominant supply share for
networking chips, and its proactive pricing strategy, which puts it in a favorable position to

benefit from rising AI investments.
We believe the overall ABF substrate market bottomed in 2H24, and growth should
accelerate from 2H26 onward with the ramp of multiple next-generation, large body size
ABF substrates for new server GPUs, CPUs, ASICs and networking chips. New
technologies, such as embedded passives and Intel's EMIB-T, should also increase
manufacturing complexity of the package substrates, adding to the overall undercapacity
as usable output decreases.

Similar to our analysis of other tech hardware companies within our coverage, we use a
residual income (RI) valuation model to value NYPCB – we think it derives the most
accurate value of the firm given that it takes into account cost of equity. Key assumptions
(all unchanged):
• Cost of equity of 9.3%;
° Risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield);
° Equity risk premium of 8.7%;
° Beta of 1.0;
• Medium-term growth rate of 17%  –  fair given our analysis of the substrate market

over the next couple of years;
• Terminal growth rate of 3% (similar to the rest of the companies under our tech
hardware coverage).
Our bull and bear case values increase by similar magnitudes, to NT$3,575 and NT
$1,020, respectively.

Base case, residual income (RI) valuation model, which we think derives the most accurate
value of the ﬁrm given that it takes into account cost of equity. We use a cost of equity of
9.2% [risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield), equity risk premium of

MS Rating
▪ We expect the ABF substrate market to
revert to undersupply from 2027 onward,
with the undersupply gap reaching 26% by
2030.
▪ This is primarily driven by AI demand, with
next-gen AI/networking chips adopting
larger body size ABF substrates.
▪ In the near to medium term, the short
supply of T-glass will be a key watch point,
but we believe this will mainly affect low-

end ABF and BT substrates, as suppliers
prioritize supply to the AI chip vendors.
▪ Our price target implies 41x 2027e P/E or

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

動態更新 IC製造 ‧ 台灣

台積電 
(2330.TW /2330 TT)

多、更多、再更多的 N3 產能擴張 
重要訊息 
台積電 3Q26 財報表現符合預期，管理層預估 2026 年營收將年增 44%。 
評論及分析  
3Q26 符合預期，4Q26 展望強勁。3Q26 毛利率 66.4%，受 N2 量產爬坡稀
釋影響，較我們預估低 1.6 個百分點，但符合公司財測。受惠營收成長及營
業費用控管，營業利益及營益率均符合預期。台積電 4Q26 營收財測為 523
億至 543 億美元，以中間值計算季增 14%，較我們先前預估高出 7%，毛
利率及營益率則預期持平，主要受惠 HPC 需求強勁及 N2 量產爬坡。 
N3 持續擴產，並評估美國亞利桑那州以外的擴產機會 。隨 4Q26 展望轉
強，台積電將 2026 年美元營收成長預估由原先 40%以上上調至 44%，並
維持全年資本支出 600 億至 640 億美元。台積電過去鮮少將單一製程產能
擴充至超過原先規劃的目標( 約每月 15 萬片)。然而，除近期宣布的三座晶圓
廠(台南 Fab 18 、亞利桑那州二廠及熊本二廠) 外，台積電再宣布於台南新增
一座 N3 晶圓廠，預計於 1H28 量產。整體而言，我們預估 N3 產能將由
2026 年底的每月 15 萬至 16 萬片，在未來兩年進一步增加 8 萬片，增幅達
50%。我們認為此次擴產反映三項需求：(1)Agentic AI 帶動的 CPU 需求；
(2)HBM4e 客製化基底晶粒；以及(3)AI ASIC 。此外，台積電亦正評估在美國
亞利桑那州以外地區擴產的可能性，例如德州，但目前尚未做出最終決定。  
不會將任何商機拱手讓給競爭對手 。管理層多次強調不會讓競爭對手取得任
何商機，體現在 N3 產能的積極擴充。此外，儘管我們預期 Google(美 )委託
Intel(美 )生產的 Humufish 目前沒有備援計劃。然而，在必要情況下，台積電
將協助客戶確保產品如期交付， 暗示若 Intel 的 EMIB-T 製程進度落後，台積
電有望進一步提升市占率。部分投資人可能擔憂台積電導入 ASML High -NA
設備的時程較競爭對手晚兩年，進而影響其技術領先地位。但我們持不同看
法，採用最新設備並不必然代表技術領先。若 在 2030 年前台積電能在不投
資昂貴設備的情況下達到相近的製程效能，反而 有望取得相較同業更顯著的
成本優勢。 
投資建議  
儘管 3Q26 財報符合預期，且我們預估 4Q26 表現將更為強勁，但在市場預
期極高下，投資人仍可能對短期毛利率走勢有所疑慮。考量未來的成長動能
及穩健的執行能力，我們建議投資人趁股價回檔時逢低布局。我們僅微幅調
整獲利預估， 但隨評價基準遞延至 2028 年，我們 將目標價上調至 3,650
元，仍基於景氣上行週期平均的 20 倍本益比，維持「增加持股」評等 。 
投資風險  
全球景氣不確定性；積極擴產導致供給過剩。  
增加持股‧維持

台灣 台積電

十月 15, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R 2

法說會重點數據 
 4Q26 財測： 營收預估為 523 億至 543 億美元，以中間值計算季增
14%；毛利率 65-67%，營益率 56.5- 58.5%。 
 2026 年展望：全年營收成長預估上調至年增 44%，維持資本支出 600
億至 640 億美元。  
 毛利率稀釋：海外擴產預計稀釋 2026 年毛利率 2 個百分點，未來幾年
稀釋幅度將擴大至 3-4 個百分點。  
 產能規劃：  
 N2 製程家族產能預計於 2027 年增加至兩倍以上。  
 台南新增 N3 晶圓廠預計於 1H27 量產，另一座 N3 晶圓廠則規劃於
1H28 量產。 
 亞利桑那州 N3 二廠已完成廠房建設，目前正進行設備搬入及安
裝，預計於 3Q27 量產，略早於原訂時程。  
 日本 N3 製程預計於 2028 年量產。 
 A14 製程已開始進行產能準備，預計於 2027 年如期試產，並於
2028 年量產。

ESG 圖表定義  
項目 定義 說明 
能源使用 
直接與間接能源消耗總量(十億焦耳) 
- 公司運營範圍內消耗之能源總量 
- 能源使用總量 = 直接能源消耗總量 + 間接能源消耗量 
- 總能源使用量包括購買的能源、生產的能源 
- 就公用事業而言，輸電/電網損耗為其業務活動之一部分，被視為總能源消耗，數據不包括
為滿足能源使用而生產的電力(公用事業為出售而生產) 
- 就公用事業而言，用於能源生產的煤、天然氣或核能等原料不屬於「總能源使用」項下

購買之再生能源 
Primary再生能源購買總量(十億焦耳) 
- 公司各種來源之能源消耗量與購買的能源中屬於自然界可再生者(太陽能、風能、水能、生
物質能、地熱能)之量 
- 如無證據顯示再生能源由公司生產，則所報告的能源數據視為購買的再生能源

再生能源使用率 再生能源占總能源消耗量之比例  
CO2約當排放量 
直接 CO2與 CO2約當排放量(公噸) 
- 公司擁有或控制的來源的直接排放量(範圍 1排放量) 
- 相關氣體：二氧化碳(CO2)、甲烷(CH4)、一氧化二氮(N2O)、氫氟碳化物(HFCS)、全氟化合物
(PFCS)、六氟化硫(SF6)、三氟化氮(NF3)

CO2約當排放量營收比 
直接 CO2與 CO2約當排放量(公噸)銷售(百萬元)比 
- 公司擁有或控制的來源的直接排放量(範圍 1排放量) 
- 相關氣體：二氧化碳(CO2)、甲烷(CH4)、一氧化二氮(N2O)、氫氟碳化物(HFCS)、全氟化合物
(PFCS)、六氟化硫(SF6)、三氟化氮(NF3)

廢棄物總量 
廢棄物總量(公噸) 
- 總廢棄物 = 無害廢棄物 + 有害廢棄物 
- 僅考慮固體廢棄物，但如液體廢棄物以公噸為單位呈報，則會將其加入求得包含液體廢棄
物之總量 
- 對於採礦、石油與天然氣等行業，tailings、廢石、煤、飛灰等廢棄物亦考慮在內

廢棄物回收率 
公司呈報的廢棄物回收率 
- 廢棄物回收率 = 廢棄物回收量/總廢棄物*100 
- 廢棄物轉化為能源或經由廢棄物焚燒產生能源視為廢棄物回收 
- 經由堆肥回收的廢棄物視為回收之廢棄物

總取水量 
總取水量(立方公尺) 
- 由呈報組織直接或經水公司等中介機構從任何水源抽取的總水量 
- 水井、城鎮/公用事業/市政用水、河水、地表水等不同之水源均予以考慮

環保支出 
環保支出總金額 
- 所有用於環境保護，或防止、減少、控制環境因素、影響、危害的投資與支出，亦包括處
置、處理、衛生、清理支出

員工流動率 
員工流動率 
- 包括任何原因(自願或非自願)離開公司的員工，如辭職、退休、自然離職/死亡、醫療失
能、冗員、裁員、重組、解僱、裁減或定期合約到期 
- 員工流動率 = (離開之員工/平均員工人數)*100 
- 平均員工人數 = (本年末員工人數 + 去年末員工人數)/2 
- 本財年末員工數 = 上個財年末員工數 + 新員工數 - 離開之員工數

女性經理 
女性經理百分比 
- 女性經理占公司經理的百分比 
- 如有不同階層之百分比，如最高階、高階、中階、初階，則會考慮中階女性經理之百分比 
- 女性經理百分比 = 女性經理人數/經理總數*100

女性員工 
女性員工百分比 
- 女性員工占公司員工總數的百分比 
- 女性員工百分比 = 女性人數/員工總數*100

教育訓練總時數 
所有員工教育訓練總時數 
- 僅考慮員工教育訓練時數 
- 包括一般員工所有類型的教育訓練(如健康與安全、環境、急難事件救援、技能與職業發展) 
- 如果數據以天為單位，則乘以 8，係假設 1天 = 8小時工作

每位員工教育訓練時數 平均每年每位員工總教育訓練時數  
資料來源：Refinitiv，凱基

### Page 1

M
Idea
TSMC  | Asia Pacific
No slowdown of AI semi
demand; reiterate OW
TSMC is further expanding capacity after considering other
constraints, such as data center build, power and other
components supply.  We think the risk of over-capacity is small,
and stay OW.
TSMC sees robust AI semi demand: Despite CSP power shortages and rising
financing costs, TSMC sees demand far above its capacity. It sees no signs of a
slowdown, which may ease some market concerns. The company did not update its

AI semi CAGR guidance (55-60%), saying it is not meaningful as  the demand is
expanding rapidly.
Comments on  wafer foundry partnership (TeraFab) and competition (Intel): For
overseas fabs, TSMC is evaluating a TeraFab partnership, but TSMC must run the
partner fab. TSMC is also considering other fab campus locations, including Texas.
We asked management about IDM outsourcing from Intel and implications for A14
capacity demand. Management said it would try to be fair to all customers. On

competition, TSMC would expand capacity and “leave no food on the table” for
competitors.
On advanced packaging, we asked whether TSMC would help customers using
Intel EMIB-T advanced packaging: The company is confident about 9x and 14x
reticle size chip support. TSMC’s principle is to help customers ramp products
smoothly, so we take it as no objection if Google/MediaTek needs to adopt CoWoS/
CoPoS in future TPU versions, as we articulated in MediaTek: TSMC CoWoS support

eases MediaTek's 2028 TPU production concerns
We raise 2027/28 capex estimates to factor in 2nm and 1.4nm expansion: The
company maintained its 2026 capex guidance (US$60-64bn) and didn’t provide
next-year capex guidance. That said, it  mentioned more than a doubling of 2nm
capacity build by 2027.  Our initial cross checks with equipment venders suggest
EUV tool bookings double in 2027 (vs. 2026), followed by 25-30% growth in 2028.
We therefore raise our 2027/28 capex assumption to US$83/92bn.

3Q26 results review – margin miss, but revenue upside: Pease see Exhibit 1  and
Exhibit 2  for our earnings review. We lower our 2026 and 2027 margin
assumptions,  offset by higher revenue. We maintain our price target at NT$3,088
and reiterate our OW rating.

TSMC 3Q26 earnings and 4Q26 guidance  review
3Q26 results and 4Q26 guidance review – margin miss, but revenue upside: 3Q26
margin of 66.4% misses our forecast of 67.5%, but was largely in-line with consensus,
given the margin dilution of 2nm node and overseas operation dilution.
The 4Q26 margin midpoint of 66% shows continued pressure. TSMC guides for 4Q26
revenue to grow 14% Q/Q at the midpoint on very strong demand, in line with our 10-15%

Q/Q expectation. Full-year revenue guidance is raised to 44% Y/Y, also similar to our
preview.

TSMC: Earnings estimate revisions
Our earnings forecasts decline 3% for 2026,  3% for  2027 and is largely unchanged  for
2028: We lower our 2026/27 earnings forecast mostly due to the margin miss of 3Q26
results and weaker than our initial expectation of 4Q26 margin guidance, and also the
wider overseas fab margin dilution into 2027. We slightly raise the revenue assumption for
2027/2028 as TSMC suggested it would speed up the 3nm/2nm capacity build, but that

also comes with larger depreciation and margin pressure when new production ramps.

Our PT is unchanged at NT$3,088. Our PT implies a 20x P/E multiple on our new 2027

EPS forecast. TSMC currently trades at 17x that estimate, close to its average NTM P/E of
16.5x since 2018. We find the stock attractive at current levels.
We continue to derive our 12-month price target (base-case scenario) using a residual
income valuation framework. Our key assumptions are unchanged, including a cost of
equity of 9.2% (beta of 1.2, equity risk premium of 6.0%, and risk-free rate of 2.0%), an
intermediate growth rate of 10.5%, and a terminal growth rate of 4%.

Our bull and bear case scenario values remain at NT$3,720 and NT$1,700, respectively.

View descriptions of Risk Rewards Themes here
NT$3,720.00
24x 2027e EPS
TSMC dominates foundry services: 1)
Breakthrough in EUV tech and materials
accelerates node migration to 3nm and
beyond. 2) Intel outsources its server CPU
production to TSMC sooner than expected.
3) Intel or Samsung exits leading-edge
foundry business. 4) New tech megatrends
such as 6G or AI drive global semi revenue

growth and increase demand for leading
edge nodes. 5) Cost per transistor decreases
further along with node migration.
NT$3,088.00
20x 2027e EPS
Leading position in leading-edge logic
foundry services with some gross margin
expansion: 1) Global semi revenue CAGR is
sustained in the high single digits in 2025-
28. 2) TSMC continues to lead in market
share in 16nm, 7nm, 5nm, 3nm, and 2nm. 3)
It continues to observe robust AI-related
demand. 4) Cost per transistor does not

decrease meaningfully in 3nm and 2nm,
while capex per k is more demanding than
for 7nm and 5nm.
NT$1,700.00
11x 2027e EPS
TSMC's dominance in foundry business
erodes: 1) Global ex-memory semi revenue
growth is weaker than expected in 2025-28.
2) Declining trailing-edge market share
causes revenue shortfall. 3) Intel's
contribution is weaker than expected. Intel
and/or Samsung successfully develop
leading-edge process technology. 4) Intel's
foundry business execution proves

successful and weighs on TSMC with order
competition. 5) Demand for 2nm is less than
expected given high transistor cost for
foundry customers.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

公司研究報告 
本報告僅供宏遠投顧內部及客戶參考，雖已力求正確與完整，但因時間及市場客觀因素改變所造成產業、市場
或個股之相關條件改變，投資人需自行考量投資之實際狀況與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任及做任何保
證。非經本公司同意，不得將本報告加以引用或轉載

1 
6805 富世達(FOSITEK)

報告日期： 2026/10/08 研究員：曾智楷 (Kevin Tseng)  
壹、訪 談／法 說 內 容 
重點摘要： 
 2Q26 營收 37.4 億，YoY +42.8% ，毛 利 率34.05%，EPS 12.39
元。 1H26 毛利率  33.13% EPS 25.77 元。  
 隨著 VR200 與 ASIC 伺服器專案陸續放量 ， 伺服器產品比重提
升，再加上新產能第四季陸續開出，公司營收、毛利將維持成
長動能。  
 2027 年受惠於 Vera Rubin 平台與 ASIC 進入放量階段，下一
世代 Vera Rubin Ultra 機櫃，預期將導入更高規格 ZQD ，將
有望帶動單櫃快接頭產值提升。

一. 2Q26 毛利率達  34.05% ，EPS 12.39 元 
富世達 2Q26 營收 37.4 億， QoQ -10.6% ，YoY +42.8% ，營收較
前一季下滑主要由於手機、伺服器產品處於新舊產品轉換期影
響。而獲利方面， 2Q26 營業毛利 13.5 億， QoQ -5.8% ，YoY 
+101.6% ，毛利率 34.05% ，QoQ +1.74ppts ，YoY +9.98ppts ，
2Q26 營業利益 10.4 億，QoQ -6.9% ，YoY +109.9% ，公司表示，
受惠於製程持續優化、伺服器產品佔比提升至五成以上，帶動毛
利率提升 。2Q26 業外淨利 0.3 億 ， 稅後淨利8.49 億，QoQ -7.4% ，
YoY +157.3% ，單季稅後 EPS 12.39 元。

二. 預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 63.08  
公司公告 9 月營收 16.13 億， MoM +5.6% ，YoY +32.2% ，3Q26
營收 45.02 億，QoQ +36.1% ，YoY +79.3% ，今年累計營收 124.3
億， YoY +47.3% 。展望 2H26 ，公司預期隨著 VR200 與 ASIC 伺
服器專案陸續放量，水冷快接頭與滑軌的出貨比重將顯著提升，
再加上新產能第四季將陸續開出，公司營收將維持成長動能。而
摺疊手機部分，受惠於客戶發布新款產品，亦將推升摺疊手機業
務回溫。此外，由於高毛利伺服器產品比重提升，公司預期毛利
率將持續攀升，且營業費用率亦將隨營收規模擴大而逐漸下降。
預估 2026 全年營收 181.8 億元， YoY +46.5% ，毛利率 35.2% ，
營利率 29.4% ，稅後淨利 43.2 億，全年稅後 EPS 63.08 元。

三. 預估 2027 年稅後 EPS 112.24 元 
展望 2027 年，受惠於 Vera Rubin 平台與 ASIC 進入主要放量階

Manifold ， 將帶動機櫃快接頭用量提升 。 此外 ， 由於晶片功耗提升 、

冷卻液流量需求增加， NVIDIA 下一世代的 Vera Rubin Ultra 機

四. 投資建議為「買進」，目標價 2920 元 
考量到 AI 伺服器水冷滲透率持續提升， VR 及 ASIC 專案的強勁
需求，公司快接頭 (QD) 產品成長動能明確，公司受惠於各項專案
的陸續發酵，將顯著推升公司伺服器產品的營收貢獻，亦有望帶
動公司營收與獲利維持成長動能。目前股價交易於 2027 年 EPS
約 21~22 倍本益比，評價仍具上修空間，給於買進投資建議，目
標價 2920 元(26x 2027 EPS) 。

貳、產 業 及 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介  
富世達 (FOSITEK) 成立於 2001 年，為奇鋐科技 (3017) 旗下子公
司，主要從事電子類、電腦、資訊通信產業等 3C 電子產品轉軸組
件之研發、製造及銷售。公司產品主要應用於電腦、電子、光學、
家電、資訊、通信、醫療設備等產品之連結軸，如商用 NB 轉軸、
工業用 NB 轉軸 、 軍事用NB 轉軸 、LCD Monitor 轉軸 、PDA 轉軸、
LCD-TV 轉軸、手機轉軸、螢幕懸臂支架、醫療懸臂支架等。公司
初期與京東方、華為三方合作生產研發摺疊手機，為華為摺疊手
機的主要開案合作廠商。近年積極投入水冷快接頭模組量產，並
也陸續送給鴻海、廣達等組裝廠進行測試，同時也通過數家水冷
板供應商認證，已成功打入輝達 GB200 與 GB300 供應鏈出貨水
冷快接頭 (QD/UQD) 與滑軌， 2025 年折疊手機佔公司整體營收的
56.4%， 筆記型電腦佔6.4%， 伺服器產品佔36.3%， 其他佔0.9%。
公司總部位於台北新莊新北產業園區，並於大陸深圳及越南設有
生產據點。公司 AI 伺服器與水冷零件主要客戶為 NVIDIA 及 CSP
大廠；手機主要客戶包含華為、 Motorola ，Google 等；筆電客戶
包含 Dell 、HP、聯想、華為。而公司主要競爭對手則包含新日興
(3376) 、兆利科技 (3548) 等。

二.產業趨勢概況  
1. 四大 CSP 上修今年資本支出達 7,450 億美元 
在生成式 AI 商機快速擴張帶動下，全球雲端基礎設施投資進入新
一輪高峰。亞馬遜、 Alphabet 和微軟認為其在先前的鉅額投資已
帶動營收明顯成長 ， 同時預估未來AI 潛在商機仍持續擴大 。 其中 ，
亞馬遜、 Alphabet 和 Meta 已陸續上調 2026 全年投資金額，且四
大雲端服務供應商 (CSP) 總資本支出將上升至 7,450 億美元新高
規模。亞馬遜、微軟、 Alphabet 與 Meta 2026 年第 1、2 季分別
為 1,306 億、1,701 億美元，同時亞馬遜、 Alphabet 和 Meta 再上
修 2026 全年資本支出金額， 2026 年資本支出預估將達 7,450 億
美元。而根據 TrendForce 最新產業研究，全球主要雲端服務供應
商(CSP) 為加速建置 AI 基礎設施，資本支出預估於 2026 年將年
增 98%，2027 年再成長 50% 至 1.3 兆美元左右，年增率收斂的主
要原因，除了反映高基期因素之外，部分可歸咎為鉅額資本支出
對四大 CSP 財務衝擊日益擴大。然而，在 AI Inference 、Agentic 
AI、自研 ASIC 和新一代 AI 模型持續推升算力需求的情況下，預
期整體 CSP 投資金額將續創新高紀錄，產業成長有望從原先以
GPU 擴張為主，轉為包含 AI server 、液冷散熱、先進封裝、高速
互聯、電源和記憶體等基礎設施全面升級的態勢。

圖二 、2024-2027 年九大 CSP 資本支出總額預估 (單位 :十億美元 )

2. 2026 年全球 AI 伺服器出貨量預計年增 31% 
根據 TrendForce 最新 AI server 產業研究，近期超大型 CSP 、
Tier-2 資料中心業者對 NVIDIA GB/VR 等整櫃式 AI server 方案的
採購意願明顯提高，且 Google 、AWS 新一代 ASIC 平台於 2026
下半年陸續放量，加上中系業者擴大採用本地 AI 方案以滿足雲端
大型語言模型 (LLM) AI 需求，促使 TrendForce 上調 2026 年 AI 
server 出貨年成長幅度，從原先的 28% 更新為近 31%。北美五大
業者 2026 年的資本支出多翻倍提升，反映出大型 CSP 廠持續擴
建 AI 資料中心、 GPU 叢集、液冷散熱等新一代基礎設施，以應對
生成式 AI 及大型模型快速成長帶來的算力需求。其中， Google 
2026 至 2027 年都將側重自家 TPU 晶片發展， 2027 年出貨量有
望繼續高速成長。 AWS 2026 年以 NVIDIA GB300 作為 GPU AI 
server 主力，自研 ASIC 也穩定出貨，預期至 2027 年量能將再擴
大。 Meta 2026 年主要採用 NVIDIA GB/VR 、AMD Helios 整櫃式
方案，自研 ASIC 布局預計至 2027 年將明顯加速，為該公司長期
降低 AI 基礎設施成本、提升推論效率的重要策略。

3.液冷散熱成高階 AI 基礎設施標配，估 2026 年滲透率達 53% 
根據 TrendForce 最新研究顯示，隨著 AI 基礎設施建置浪潮及高
階晶片運算功率不斷攀升，單晶片熱設計功耗已超越 1kW ，使液
冷散熱技術正式成為高階 AI 基礎設施的標準配置。在 NVIDIA 、
AMD 與 Google 等巨頭的高階晶片與整櫃式方案強力推動下，預
估 2026 年液冷散熱於 AI 晶片的滲透率將從 2025 年的 33% 大幅
躍升至 53%，並在 2027 年進一步逼近 60%。其中， Google 因積
極導入自研 TPU 並率先全面推廣整櫃式液冷設計，成為主要推手
之一。此外，隨著 NVIDIA Vera Rubin 平台全面導入全液冷架構，
水冷板、分歧管及冷卻分配系統等關鍵零組件需求隨之大增，帶

4. 蘋果入局帶動摺疊機高成長，估今年全球摺疊機出貨年增 24% 
根據 Counterpoint Research 報告 ， 全球摺疊智慧型手機累計出貨
量預計將於 2026 年底突破 1 億支大關，達成自 2019 年首度問世
以來的重大里程碑。隨著消費者對大尺寸書本式摺疊機的需求攀
升，加上蘋果 (Apple) 推出首款摺疊 iPhone 的帶動，預估 2026 年
全球摺疊機出貨量將年增 24% ，2027 年出貨預估年增 37% 。而
根據 Counterpoint Research 預估 ， 三星(Samsung) 憑藉 Galaxy Z 
Fold8 等大尺寸書本式設計的強勁動能 ， 預計在2026 年將以  38%
的市占率穩居全球龍頭寶座；而蘋果 (Apple) 推出的首款摺疊
iPhone ，雖預期在初期會面臨產能限制，但仍將為高階市場帶來
立即的衝擊，預估 2026 年就能迅速搶下 25% 的市占率空降全球
第二；而華為 (Huawei) 則預估以 22% 的市占率位居第三，主要由
於其在中國市場依然保持強勢的主導地位，並受到  Pura X Max 
等產品的推動。

Document 205: [207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 신규 공장이 채운 실적, 수주가 더할 모멘텀삼성바이오로직스(207940) 신규 공장이 채운 실적, 수주가 더_21961458

Source PDF: all report pdf/[207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 신규 공장이 채운 실적, 수주가 더할 모멘텀삼성바이오로직스(207940) 신규 공장이 채운 실적, 수주가 더_21961458.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-07
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
미국 록빌·5공장 가세로 3분기 기대치 부합 전망 
삼성바이오로직스의 2026년 3분기 연결 매출액은 1조 3,637억 원(+8.4% Y oY), 
영업이익은 6,092억 원(-3.8% YoY, 영업이익률 44.7%)으로 시장 기대치에 부합할 
전망이다. 5월 파업에 따른 생산 차질에도 1~4공장(총 60.4만 리터)의 풀가동과 
신규 공장의 매출 기여가 외형 성장을 뒷받침할 것으로 예상한다. 미국 록빌 
공장(6만 리터)은 3분기부터 매출 기여가 본격화되고, 5공장(18만 리터)도 
인증용(PPQ) 배치 생산을 통해 매출이 확대될 전망이다. 2분기에는 미국 공장·5공장 
관련 비용이 선반영됐으나, 3분기에는 매출 증가에 따른 고정비 부담 완화로 
영업이익률이 전분기 대비 0.3%p 상승할 것으로 예상한다. 
항체 중심 CDMO에서 펩타이드로 확장 
삼성바이오로직스의 2026년 신규 수주는 약 1.2조 원으로 전년 수주액의 약 20% 
수준에 그치고 있으나, 연말부터 내년까지 수주 모멘텀은 점차 강화될 것으로 
판단한다. 2025년 하반기 이후 미국의 의약품 관세 불확실성이 완화되며 글로벌 
제약사와의 논의가 늘어나고 있어, 신규 수주 계약으로 이어질 것으로 기대된다. 
11월 유상증자 납입이 마무리되면 6공장(18만 리터) 착공과 스위스 폴리펩타이드 
인수가 본격화되며 중장기 성장 기대감도 높아질 전망이다. 특히 폴리펩타이드 
인수가 연내 완료되면 2027년부터 연결 실적에 반영되는 동시에, 항체 의약품 
중심의 CDMO 사업을 GLP-1 등 펩타이드로 확장하는 계기가 될 전망이다. 
목표주가 200만 원으로 하향, 수주 회복이 관건 
삼성바이오로직스의 목표주가를 209만 원에서 200만 원으로 하향한다. 유상증자 
신주 발행에 따른 주당가치 4.7% 희석 효과를 반영했다. 폴리펩타이드 인수 효과는 
거래완료 이후 실적 추정치에 반영할 예정이다. 노조 파업 불확실성이 주가에 
부담으로 작용할 수 있어 신규 수주 모멘텀의 회복이 필요한 시점이다. 
목표주가 (하향) 2,000,000원

2026.09.11  
HD 현대중공업 (329180)

드디어 엔진 증설, 저평가된 주가  Flash Note 
[조선/기계/방산] 변용진 2122-9181 yongjin.byun@imfnsec.com

Check Point  Buy (Maintain) 
HD현대중공업 중형엔진 및 SMR 공장 증설 결정  
엔진도 끌어올리지 못하는 조선업 주가, 그래도 펀더멘털은 굳건

목표주가(12M) 860,000 원(유지) 
종가(2026.09.10) 453,500 원 
상승여력 89.6 %

HD현대중공업, 중형엔진 및 SMR 시설투자 공시  
기다리던 엔진 증설의 세부 사항이 드디어 공개되었다. HD 현대중공업은 10일 공시를 통해 총 1조 722억원(중형엔진 
8,336억원/SMR 2,386억원) 규모의 시설투자 계획을 밝혔다. 먼저 중형엔진 관련, 글로벌 전력 수요 증가에 따른 엔진발전 시장 
확대에 대응 목적으로 생산능력 확충을 위한 토지 취득 및 공장 신설 및 생산설비 구축에 28년 5월까지 투자가 진행될 예정이다. 
중형엔진의 생산 능력은 현재 연간 3GW급에서 증설이 완료되면 7.2GW급으로 확장되며, 첫 엔진 출하는 28년 하반기로 예상된다. 
증설되는 신공장의 가동률은 28년 30%로 출발하여 30년 100%로 올라설 전망이다. 이에 따라 연간 1GW당 약 1조원의 매출과 
20%이상의 영업이익(현 엔진사업부 이상) 수준으로 추정되며, 계획대로 증설이 완료되고 일감을 확보한다면 총 4조원 이상의 매출 
증대 효과가 기대된다. SMR 투자 관련해서는 SMR 주기기 제작사업 진입 및 수주물량 증가에 대응하기 위해 SMR 전용 공장, 설비 
구축 및 기존 공장 최적화에 29년 4월까지 2,386억원이 투입된다. 이는 연간으로 Terrapower의 Natrium SMR 주기기 2기(각 
345MW)를 제작할 수 있는 규모다. HD현대중공업은 이번 증설이 데이터센터용 엔진만을 위한 것이 아님을 강조했다. 데이터센터 뿐 
아니라 국가전력망, 원전용 비상발전기 등 전세계적으로 증가하는 육상용 발전엔진 수요에 대응하기 위함이며, 최근 미국의 육상 
데이터센터 사업이 님비현상 등으로 일부 좌초되고 있음에도 그보다 더 큰 그림에 베팅한 것이다.  
엔진도 끌어올리지 못하는 조선업 주가, 그래도 펀더멘털은 굳건  
분명한 기업가치 증분의 근거가 되는 증설공시였음에도 불구하고 HD현대중공업의 주가는 발표 이후 하락으로 마감하였다. 올해 
조선업 주가는 YTD 평균 10%이상 하락하며 약세를 보이고 있는 상태다. (HD현대중공업 -10.0%, 삼성중공업 -11.6%, 한화오션 -
25.5%)  
조선사 공히 2분기의 실적 서프라이즈와 함께 올해 수주목표를 사실상 초과 달성하는 등 우수한 펀더멘털을 유지하고 있으나 이는 
주가 상승재료로 반영되고 있지 않다. 7~8월 반도체발 주식시장 혼란에 따른 전체적 수급 약세도 무시할 수 없는 요인이다. 여기에 
지난해 주가 상승의 주요 동력으로 작용하였던 미국 함정 사업 관련 협력 진전이 느린 점 등이 주가 약세의 주요 원인으로 
분석된다. 백악관 및 美해군에서는 지속적으로 함정 해외 건조의 필요성을 역설하고 있으나 美입법부에서는 지역구 의원의 반대 
등으로 관련 법안의 입법 동력이 약해져 있는 상황이다. 美국회의 이번 회기(119대 회기, ~2027년 1월 3일까지)가 이미 막바지에 
다다르고 있으며 11월 중간선거 일정까지 감안하면 실질적으로 이번 회기중에 관련 법안이 통과 및 발효될 가능성은 미미하기 
때문에, 2026년 남은 하반기에 함정사업 관련 조선업 주가 동력을 기대하기는 힘들어졌다. 다가오고 있는 3분기 실적도 원화 강세 
및 여름휴가/사고 등에 따른 조업일수 하락, 후판 유통가 상승 등 감익 요인이 산적해 있어 2분기에 비해 쉽지 않은 상황이다.  
그러나 여전히 본업인 조선업의 체력은 훌륭하다. 전세계 선박 발주량은 8월 누적기준 5,972만 CGT로 전년동기 대비 +60.6% 많은 
발주가 나오고 있으며 이는 2021년 이후 가장 강한 발주세다. 한국 조선사의 주력 선종인 LNG, 탱커, 컨테이너선의 발주 비중이 
전체 발주량의 67%를 차지하고 있으며 이에 힘입어 조선사들은 수주목표를 사실상 초과 달성하였다. (상선기준, HD현대중공업 달성 
130.6억 달러/목표 114.7억, 삼성중공업 달성 61억/목표 57억, 한화오션 달성 60억/목표없음) 3분기 실적은 다소 쉬어갈 수 
있으나 여전히 수주잔고에 기반한 추정상 매출/영업이익은 2028년까지 꾸준한 상승이 예상된다. 작년보다 오히려 하락한 주가가 
현저한 저평가일 수밖에 없는 이유다. 조선업 비중확대 및 HD현대중공업 Top Pick 의견을 유지한다.

Document 220: AI Scare Raises Growth Fears and Lowers Bond Yields

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI Scare Raises Growth Fears and Lowers Bond Yields.pdf
Report source: researchmail轉寄
Date: 2026-10-16
Pages: 8
Status: ok

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: AI Scare Raises Growth Fears and Lowers Bond Yields
Date:    4 hours ago
AI Scare Raises Growth Fears and Lowers Bond Yields

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
AI Scare Raises Growth Fears
and Lowers Bond Yields
By Ed Yardeni & Elias Griepentrog • 15 Oct 2026
View in browser
Photo by Jonny Gios / Unsplash

I. Treasury Yields
US Treasury yields fell sharply today, extending Wednesday's retreat from
intraday highs. The 10-year yield dropped 9bps to 5.10%, while the 30-
year fell 10bps to 5.43% (chart).

### Page 2

Even the 2-year yield declined slightly to 4.77% (chart).
Remarkably, this broad-based decline occurred despite two hotter-than-
expected inflation reports. Normally, such surprises would push Treasury
yields higher as investors priced in a more hawkish Fed.
So, what's going on? We think the bond market is focusing on the
sustainability of the AI investment boom. That issue came to the fore this
week as CoreWeave's bonds plunged below 80 cents on the dollar and

debt financing major data center projects also tumbled. A new Gartner
report estimates AI data center operators will need $8.5 trillion in revenues
through 2030 to cover costs and earn healthy returns, versus projected
revenues of "just" $3.6 trillion.

### Page 3

Why does this matter? The AI buildout has become a major engine of US
economic growth. But doubts about future returns are pushing up
borrowing costs, potentially constraining access to capital and ultimately
slowing investment spending.
As a result, investors may be lowering their economic growth expectations
amid concerns that the AI boom could slow, pushing Treasury yields lower.
This reinforces our longstanding view that the earlier surge in Treasury

yields largely reflected stronger-than-expected nominal GDP growth
(chart).
We are nowhere near ready to write off the AI investment boom. Demand
for compute continues to outpace supply, while rising adoption should fuel
further investment. Indeed, just five hyperscalers (Amazon, Alphabet,
Meta, Microsoft, and Oracle) are expected to spend a combined $892
billion this year and $1.1 trillion next year. Their strong balance sheets and
cash flow generation make their spending plans relatively insensitive to

higher borrowing costs. While heavily indebted AI infrastructure providers
may struggle, we expect the hyperscalers to keep spending and the
Roaring 2020s to keep roaring.
II. Inflation
Let's take a closer look at September's hotter-than-expected PPI report
and what it tells us about inflationary pressures in the pipeline.

### Page 4

(1) PPI for final demand. September's PPI final demand surged 1.3%
m/m, largely reflecting a 10.8% spike in energy prices. However, even
excluding the volatile food, energy, and trade services components,
producer prices still rose 0.5%, suggesting that inflationary pressures
remain broad-based.
On a y/y basis, core PPI final demand rose to 5.0%, with services and
goods prices rising 5.0% and 5.2%, respectively (chart).
(2) PPI for consumption. The PPI for personal consumption, a key input

into the Fed’s preferred PCED inflation gauge, surged by 1.3% m/m in
September (chart). Excluding food and energy, it was up 0.6%.

### Page 5

Core PPI personal consumption rose to 4.6% y/y and remained well
above both core PCED and core CPI inflation, suggesting that upside
risks to consumer inflation remain elevated (chart).
(3) Supercore inflation. The PPI supercore inflation rate rose to 4.6% y/y
in September (chart).

### Page 6

(4) Core PCED. With September CPI and PPI now in hand, most
forecasters expect core PCED inflation to rise 0.3% m/m in September,
which would lift the y/y rate from 3.0% to 3.1%. The Cleveland Fed’s
Inflation Nowcasting model projects 0.28% m/m and 3.05% y/y,
respectively.
III. Booming Economy
For now, the US economy shows no signs of slowing down. Consider the
latest data points:
(1) Jobless claims. Initial jobless claims edged up slightly to 199,000 in

the week ended October 10, the fifth straight week below 200,000. That is
the longest streak since 1969! Continuing claims declined to 1.71 million,
dragging down the 4-week moving average to the lowest level since April
2023 (chart).

### Page 7

(2) Retail sales. September retail sales rose 0.8% m/m, partly driven by a
6.4% surge in gasoline station sales amid elevated pump prices (chart).
Still, eight of the 13 major categories posted gains. Sales at food services
and drinking places, the only services category and a key discretionary
spending indicator, jumped 1.1% m/m, their eighth consecutive monthly
gain. They rose 7.0% y/y, the strongest increase since March 2023.
(3) GDP. Reflecting today's retail sales report, the Atlanta Fed’s GDPNow

model estimates Q3 real GDP growth at 3.6% (saar), unchanged from
October 8 (chart). Consumer spending is projected to rise 3.6%. AI-related
demand is boosting business equipment investment (17.6%), fixed
business structures (7.0%), and intellectual property investment (6.6%).
Final sales to private domestic purchasers are expected to grow 4.7%.

### Page 8

clause.

Document 223: AIの雇用への影響をどう読むか

Source PDF: all report pdf/AIの雇用への影響をどう読むか.pdf
Report source: DIR
Date: 2026-10-14

### Page 1

米国

株式会社大和総研 丸の内オフィス 〒100-6756 東京都千代田区丸の内一丁目 9 番 1 号 グラントウキョウ ノースタワー

2026 年 10 月 14 日 全13 頁

AI の雇用への影響をどう読むか 
一部の職種で狭まる若手の「入口」、経験を積む場をどう残すか

AI アナリティックリサーチ室 主任研究員 新田 尭之

[要約] 
 近年の実証研究では、米国を中心に、生成AI の影響を受けやすい一部の職種で若手の
雇用悪化が相次いで報告されている。悪化は主に採用の減少として表れており、最初
の職を得る「入口」が狭まっている。ただし、悪化の一部は ChatGPT 公開前に始まっ
ており、在宅勤務の広がりやコロナ禍の採用増の反動とも重なる。複数の要因が考え
られる中、AI の影響の大きさについて明確な結論は得られていない。 
 若手の「入口」が狭まる一方、米国全体では雇用者数が増えている。もっとも、 雇用
増の多くは民間教育・医療によるもので、幅広い産業には広がっていない。採用も離
職も少ないため、既に職に就いている人は職を失いにくいが、これから職を得る人や
転職を望む人にとっては空席が少ない。さらに、AI による省力化は、人員を減らすよ
り、業務の中身や外注を見直す形で進む例が多い。雇用者数だけでは、こうした仕事
の機会の変化は見えにくい。 
 今後の雇用を分けるのは、AI による値下げや品質向上で需要が広がったり、新しい業
務が生まれたりして、どれだけ人手が必要になるかである。ただし、新たな仕事が生
まれても、若手に回るとは限らない。41 カ国の多国籍企業の分析では、生成AI を導入
した企業の拠点で経験者の雇用が増え、若手の比率が下がった。経験者の多くは、入
口の仕事を通じて育つ。若手の採用を絞る判断は、個々の企業には合理的でも、続け
ば将来の経験者が育たない「育成の空洞化」を招きかねない。若手が経験を積む場を
どう残すかが問われており、社内で人を育てる日本企業も無縁ではいられないだろう。

### Page 2

1

2 / 13

1. はじめに 
近年、生成 AI の進化や普及を背景に、AI が雇用に与える影響について、米国を中心に実証
研究が蓄積されている（図表1）。 
図表 1 生成 AI が雇用に与える影響に関する近年の主な実証研究

職種。

　時間を節約できた利用者の85％は他の業務に充てると回答

### Page 3

1

3 / 13

例えば、スタンフォード大学の研究者らは、給与計算・人事サービスを手掛ける ADP の給与
記録を用いて米国の労働市場を分析し、主に次の 6 点を報告している 1。①AI による経済全体
にわたる広範な雇用喪失は確認されない。②一方、AI の影響を受けやすい職種では、受けにく
い職種に比べて22～25 歳の雇用の伸びが弱く、年長層には同程度の差はみられない。③この若
年層の職種間格差は2026 年半ばにかけて拡大を続けた。④その差は主に離職の増加ではなく、
採用の減少による。⑤雇用の減少はAI が人の作業を代替する使い方の多い職種に集中し、仕事
を補完する使い方では雇用の横ばい・増加との関連がみられる。 ⑥調整は基本給よりも雇用の
面に表れている。 
こうした結果は、AI が雇用に与える影響を、全体の人数だけでなく、年齢や採用・離職、AI
の使われ方などに分けて捉える必要性を示している。そこで、本稿ではこうした実証研究や統
計データを基に、①雇用の悪化が目立つ層と、そのうちAI の影響と読める範囲（第 2 章） 、②
雇用者数だけでは捉えにくい仕事の機会の変化（第3 章 ）、 ③今後の雇用を分ける要因（第4 章）
を検討する。

2. 悪化は「入口」で目立つが、AI だけでは説明できない 
（1）AI の影響を受けやすい職種の若年層を中心に悪化 
近年の実証研究を見ると、米国の若年層で雇用の悪化が目立つ。前述のスタンフォード大学
の研究では、22～25 歳の雇用者数は2026 年6 月にChatGPT 公開時の2022 年11 月を約2％下回
った一方、26 歳以上の各年齢層では増加し、特に35～40 歳では約11％多い（図表2）。 
図表 2 年齢層別に見た雇用者数の推移（米国、2022 年 11 月＝1）

（注）ADP の給与記録のうち、2021 年 1 月～2026 年 6 月に継続して観測される企業の 70 歳未満のフルタイム労
働者（毎月 350 万～500 万人） 。年齢層別人口の変化は未調整。 
（出所）Brynjolfsson, Chandar, and Chen [2026]より大和総研作成（画像の加工・仮訳は大和総研）

1 Brynjolfsson, Chandar, and Chen [2026]. AI 曝露はEloundou et al. [2024] の指標のうち GPT-4 による

ただし、若年層の雇用が職種を問わず悪化したわけではない。同研究の職種別の推計では、
AI への曝露（仕事の内容がAI で代替・支援されうる度合い）が最も高い職種群の22～25 歳の
雇用の伸びは、2022 年11 月から2026 年6 月までに、最も低い職種群の雇用の伸びを約18％ポ
イント下回った。若年層の中でも、AI の影響を受けやすい職種で悪化が目立つ。 
さらに、米国国勢調査局の研究者らは、大学の卒業記録と雇用の行政記録を 接続し、専攻別
に卒業生の就職状況を比べた。これによると、 AI への曝露が最も高い専攻の卒業生は、
ChatGPT 公開直後から、卒業翌四半期に就業している割合が曝露の低い専攻の卒業生と比べて
低下し始め、2024 年以降は約5％ポイント低い2。 
米国の約6,500 万人分の職歴データを企業単位で分析した研究でも、生成AI の導入企業では
若手（職位の低い層）の雇用が2023 年初めから6 四半期で比較対象の企業より約9％減った。
これも主に採用の減少によるという 3。求人の側では、求人サイト Indeed の分析で、テック職
で 5 年以上の経験を求める割合の上昇が確認されている4。 
ニューヨーク連銀が集計したデータからも、若年大卒者の悪化は確認できる。22～27 歳の若
年大卒者の失業率は、2018 年までほぼ一貫して全労働者を下回っていたが、2019 年以降はおお
むね上回るようになり、2026 年 6 月は 5.7％と全労働者（4.1％）を 1.6％ポイント上回る。大
卒でない若年層（22～27 歳）の失業率が 2025 年秋を頂点に下がり始めた後も、若年大卒者の
失業率は高止まりしている（図表3）5。

なお、オンラインのフリーランス市場でも、AI の影響を受ける分野では、補完的とみなされ
る技能の分野を含め、経験の浅い人向けの案件が、影響を受けない分野に比べて相対的に減っ
たと報告されている 6。年齢と経験の浅さは同じ区分ではないが、若手の 雇用悪化と経験の浅
い人向けの案件の減少は、最初の仕事を得る機会、いわば「入口」が狭まっている点で共通す
る。 
（2）ChatGPT 公開前からの悪化や在宅勤務の広がりなども重なる 
ただし、こうした悪化の原因すべてを生成AI の影響に帰するのは早計であろう。第一に、悪
化の一部は ChatGPT 公開前に始まっていた。例えば、米国のソフトウェア開発職の求人は、
2022 年 2 月をピークに急減し、ChatGPT 公開時には既にピークを3 分の 1 あまり下回っていた
（図表5）。

相対的な減少は2022 年より前に始まっており、2022 年以降に傾向の明確な変化は見られない。

第二に、在宅勤務の広がりである。ニューヨーク連銀のブログに掲載された分析によると、
米国の在宅勤務が可能な職業では、若手と年長の大卒者の失業率の差が2019 年から 2024 年に
約 1.4％ポイント広がった 8。これは、在宅勤務が難しい職業の同期間の拡大幅（約0.5％ポイ
ント）を大きく上回る。同分析は、在宅勤務では若手への OJT や上司からの助言が難しくなる
ため、企業が経験の浅い人材の採用をためらいやすくなると指摘している。 米英加豪の採用記
録約 2.4 億件の分析でも、在宅勤務のしやすさを同時に考慮すると、AI 曝露と採用者に占める
若手（職位の低い層）の比率との関係は大きく弱まり、多くの場合、統計的に有意 な関係は確
認できなくなった 9。 
米国外の研究も、若手の雇用の減少を AI だけでは説明していない。デンマークでは、AI 曝
露の高いいくつかの職で若手の雇用が減ったものの、研究者らはその減少はAI チャットボット
を導入した企業によるものではないとしている 10。さらに、韓国銀行の研究者の分析によれば、
韓国では 2022 年 6 月から 2026 年 6 月に 15～29 歳の雇用が 28.5 万人減り、その 94％は AI の
影響を受けやすい産業で生じた 11。同研究は、コロナ禍の採用増の反動や経験者中心の採用、
在宅勤務の広がりなども作用した可能性があると指摘している。

層）向け・経験者向けの区分は Lightcast が求人の記載から判定したもの。求人の数の分析であり、実際に誰
が採用されたかは捉えない点で、第 2 章（1）の研究とは対象が異なる。

そして先に紹介したスタンフォード大学の研究では 、在宅勤務や金利への感応度を調整して
も、22～25 歳の高曝露職と低曝露職の雇用の伸びの差は大きく変わらないが、学歴構成も含め
て調整するとその差は調整前の約 18％ポイントから約 8％ポイントに縮み、統計的に有意な関
係は確認できなくなる12。 
以上のように、一部の職種で若手の雇用が悪化していることは確認できるが、その原因を生
成 AI に求めるには慎重さが必要である。その影響を見極めるには、ChatGPT 公開前からの悪化
と、公開後に加わった可能性のある変化を分けて考える必要がある。また 、足元の弱さをコロ
ナ禍の採用増の反動だけで説明できるとは限らない。複数の要因が考えられる中で、本稿で取
り上げた研究でも、AI の影響の大きさについて明確な結論は得られていない。

3. 雇用者数だけでは捉えにくい仕事の機会の変化 
仕事を得る機会を考えるには、雇用者数が増えているかどうかに加え、どの分野で増えてい
るのか、新たな採用がどの程度行われているのかを見る必要がある。また、AI による省力化も、
人員数を変えずに業務や外注の見直しとして進む場合がある。以下では、これら三つの側面を
確認する。 
（1）雇用増は民間教育・医療に偏る 
米国では、ChatGPT が公開された2022 年 11 月から2026 年 8 月までに、情報や専門・事業サ
ービスなど、AI の影響を受けやすい職種の多い産業で雇用は減少した。一方、全体の雇用増を
支えたのは民間教育・医療である。同期間の雇用増約 480 万人のうち、約 3 分の 2（約 325 万
人）を占めた。ただし、直近 1 年の全体の雇用増は約 60 万人にとどまり、そのうち約 54 万人
を民間教育・医療が占める（図表6）。 
図表 6 業種別に見た雇用者数の増減（米国）

（注）その他は全体から掲載した業種を差し引いた残差。 
（出所）米労働統計局（BLS）より大和総研作成

12 この研究では、学歴構成は AI が仕事に影響する経路も反映している可能性があるため、その違いを調整し
て差が縮んでも、AI 以外の要因で説明できたとは限らない、としている。

民間教育・医療の雇用者数の伸びは、コロナ禍で落ち込んだ反動にもみえる。ただ、コロナ
禍前の2020 年2 月から見ても、雇用増約680 万人の半分（約340 万人）を占めており、雇用増
の最大の担い手であることは変わらない。全体の雇用増が、幅広い産業での雇用拡大を意味す
るわけではない。 
（2）低採用・低離職の下で就職・転職の機会は限られる 
雇用者数の動きと、新たに職を得る機会の違いは、採用と離職からも確認できる。 米国の求
人労働異動調査（JOLTS）によると、2026 年 1～7 月の採用率は平均 3.3％と、コロナ禍前の
2017～19 年（3.8％）を下回り、失業率が 7～8％台だった 2012～13 年並みに低い（図表 7）。
同じ期間の比較では、離職率も3.7％から3.3％に下がり、自己都合退職率（2.2％→2.0％）も
解雇率（1.2％→1.1％）も低下した。採用率の低さは若手だけの現象ではなく、労働市場全体
にみられる。 
図表 7 採用率と離職率（米国）

（出所）BLS より大和総研作成

離職が少なければ、欠員を埋める採用も生まれにくい。就職・転職の機会が限られる中、 先
行きへの不安から今の職にとどまる動きは「ジョブハギング（仕事にしがみつくこと） 」と呼
ばれ、2025 年 8 月ごろから米国の報道で広く取り上げられている13。米国の履歴書作成サイト
が2026 年2 月に行った調査では、自らをジョブハギングの状態にあると回答した就業者が57％
と、半年前の 45％から増えた。その 7 割は、今後半年以内に AI が自分の雇用に影響すること
を懸念していた 14。就職の入口に立つ人にとっては、いわば「空席の少ない市場」である。 
（3）AI による省力化の影響は、人員の削減より仕事の中身の見直しに表れている 
AI による省力化についても、雇用者数だけでは変化を捉えにくい。OECD が日本を含む7 カ国

13 例えば、Fortune “Workers are ‘job hugging’ in a stagnant labor market, but growing resentment 
means they could bail as soon as the next Great Resignation comes”（2025 年8 月 18 日）。 
14 ResumeBuilder.com “6 in 10 Workers Are Clinging To Their Jobs as Job Hugging Soars in 2026”
（2026 年2 月11 日）。米国の就業者2,188 人へのオンライン調査で、ジョブハギングを、会社への愛着では
なく不安から今の職にとどまることとしている。

の中小企業に行った調査では、生成 AI を使う企業の 83％が人員の必要数に影響はないと答え
た。必要な人員数の減少（9％）より、従業員の業務負担（33％）や外注への依存（14％）の
減少を挙げる企業が多い 15。社内の人員数が変わらなくても、外部に依頼する仕事は減りうる。 
前出のデンマークの研究では、時間を節約できた利用者の 85％が、その時間を他の業務に充
てると答え、雇用主が特段の施策を講じていない職場でも、利用者の8％が AI の出力の品質確
認など全く新しい業務を担っていた。研究者らは、こうした状況が、業務の見直しなど計測さ
れにくい投資が先行し、生産性の伸びが遅れて表れる「J カーブ」の初期段階と整合的だとし
ている 16。AI による省力化が、直ちに人員や労働時間の削減につながるのではなく、仕事の配
分を変える形で進んでいることがうかがえる。

4. 新たな仕事が生まれても、若手に回るとは限らない 
第 2 章で紹介した研究では、若手の雇用悪化は、主に「解雇」ではなく「採用されない」形
で表れている。この状態が続くかは、AI による省力化で生まれる余力を企業がどの業務に振り
向け、新たな需要がどれだけ人手を必要とするかにかかっている。 
省力化の後の展開は、主に三つの経路として整理できる 17。①人数を変えずに仕事の中身が
変わる。②既存の社員で対応できる範囲が広がり、採用・外注の抑制や人員削減につながる。
③値下げや品質向上による需要の拡大、新しい業務の創出が人手を必要とし、配置転換や採用
につながる。 
AI 導入後の雇用の動きには、企業規模による違いも見られる。デンマークの研究では、AI を
導入した小規模企業で新たな採用が抑えられ、雇用の増えるペースが鈍ったと報告されている。
一方、大規模企業では、同様の雇用の伸びの鈍化は確認されなかった18。 
②と③は、同じ企業の中で同時に進みうる。41 カ国の多国籍企業を対象とした研究は、AI導
入企業で増えた雇用がどの層に向かったかを示している。生成AI を導入した企業の拠点では、
ChatGPT 公開直後から若手（職位の低い層）の比率が下がり始め、2026 年 3 月には導入してい
ない企業の拠点での比率を 1.9％ポイント下回った（図表 8）19。この比率低下の主因は、若手

15 OECD [2025]. 対象はオーストリア、カナダ、ドイツ、アイルランド、日本、韓国、英国の従業員249 人以
下の企業 5,232 社（2024 年10～12 月）。割合は生成AI を利用する企業の回答で、一時点の自己申告による。

ではない。

一致するとは限らないとしている。日本も対象に含まれるが、データが日本の雇用を捉える割合は 2％程度に

の職歴データを用いて米国の導入企業で若手の雇用減を示しており、対象や推計方法により結果は異なる。

の雇用減少ではなく、ChatGPT 公開から約 1 年半後に始まった経験者の雇用増加である。この

（出所）Chandar and Klein Teeselink [2026] より大和総研作成（画像の加工・仮訳は大和総研）

③の経路を示す研究もある。米国の上場企業では、AI 投資を増やした企業ほど、主に製品の
革新を通じて売上と雇用が伸びたとされる 20。デンマークの研究で報告された、AI の出力の品
質確認などの新しい業務も③の芽といえる。 
採用の構成も、AI の使い方によって変わりうる。AI を使う既存社員が若手の入口の仕事を担
えば、採用は経験者に寄りうる。逆に、AI の支援で経験の浅い人に任せられる仕事が増えれば、

20 Babina et al. [2024]. 2010～2018 年の非テック上場企業の分析。効果は規模の大きい企業に集中する。他
社からの需要の移転も含みうるが、AI 投資の多い産業では産業全体でも売上と雇用が増えたとされる。

### Page 11

1

11 / 13

若手を採る利点は高まりうる。AI が経験の差を縮めるこうした作用は、「専門性の平準化」と
呼ばれる 21。 
もっとも、これまでの実証研究では、採用が経験者に寄る動きが目立つ。この 動きが続けば、
懸念されるのは「育成の空洞化」である。 経験者の多くは、入口の仕事を通じて育つ。若手採
用の絞り込みは、個々の企業には合理的でも、経済全体では将来の経験者層 を薄くしかねない。
AI の支援と経験者の指導を組み合わせ、若手が経験を積む場を残す工夫が求められる。 
日本企業もこの問題と無縁ではいられないだろう。日本では少子高齢化で人手不足が続き、
新卒を一括採用して社内で育てる企業が多い。このため、影響は採用数より、採る職種の構成
や、人手の足りない部署への配置転換に先に表れる可能性がある 22。とはいえ、採用数が保た
れても、AI が入口の仕事を担えば、若手が経験を積む場は細りうる。問われているのは「AI で
若手の仕事をどこまで省けるか」ではなく、 「AIを使いながら若手をどう育てるか」である。

以上

Document 274: Continue To Proceed With Caution

Source PDF: all report pdf/Continue To Proceed With Caution.pdf
Report source: researchmail轉寄
Date: 2026-10-15
Pages: 9
Status: ok

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: Continue To Proceed With Caution
Date:    8 hours ago
Continue To Proceed With Caution

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
Continue To Proceed With
Caution
By Ed Yardeni & Elias Griepentrog • 14 Oct 2026
View in browser
Photo by Muhammad Daudy / Unsplash

I. Risk Off Is Back
On September 15, we advised our readers to "Proceed With Caution." We
postponed our year-end S&P 500 target of 8,400 to mid-2027. Our
concern isn't earnings; it's stock valuations.

### Page 2

We remain bullish on the economy and earnings, expecting no recession
through the end of the decade in 2029. Accordingly, we haven’t lowered
our 2027 S&P 500 earnings forecast of $425 per share, which is only
slightly above the industry analysts' current consensus of $423.08 (chart).
Instead, we lowered our year-end forward P/E forecast from 19.8 to 18.6;
that’s what reduced our year-end S&P 500 target to 7,900. So far, our
caution has been warranted. The S&P 500's forward P/E has been moving

toward our targeted valuation multiple (chart).
Risk-off sentiment was on full display in the stock market today for most of
the reasons we flagged in mid-September. The S&P 500 fell 1.1% to
7,718.94, while the Nasdaq Composite and Russell 2000 both dropped
1.9%. Selling pressure was particularly pronounced among technology

### Page 3

stocks, with the SOXX tumbling 4.5% (chart). Interestingly, IGV fell 3.0%,
while MAGS was down only 1.9%.
The AI trade is getting sloppy amid intensifying competition and doubts
about returns on massive AI investments. Shares of heavily indebted
neocloud provider CoreWeave plunged 16% today amid growing concerns
about its ability to service its debt (chart). Furthermore, today's CPI was
hotter than expected, raising the odds of two Fed rate hikes over the rest

of this year. A cargo ship sank in the Strait of Hormuz after hitting an
Iranian mine.
Meanwhile, the risk-off sentiment we anticipated may be helping
Treasuries find their footing. On Saturday, we suggested that the 10-year
Treasury yield might stabilize around 5.25%. So far, that call is holding up

### Page 4

despite mounting fears of further Fed tightening today (chart). That's partly
because Treasuries offer attractive real returns, with the 10-year TIPS
yielding around 2.90% (chart).
Our worry list remains long, including more partisanship after the midterm
elections and a re-escalation of the war in the Middle East. We expect
risk-off sentiment to persist, further compressing valuation multiples.
That's why we still recommend proceeding with caution. On the other

hand, buying opportunities are increasing, and Santa could still deliver a
year-end rally up to 7,900.
II. Inflation Is Back Too
Today's September CPI report fueled bets on further Fed tightening. The
probability of an October rate hike jumped from 21% to 36%, briefly
reaching 45%. Meanwhile, the 2-year Treasury yield surged as much as
13bps intraday before closing 3bps higher at 4.79%, roughly 95 bps above
the midpoint of the federal funds target range (chart).

### Page 5

Here's more:
(1) CPI inflation (m/m). The headline CPI jumped 0.6% m/m in
September, its biggest increase since March, led by a 9.3% surge in
energy prices (chart). But inflationary pressures were broad-based, with
the core CPI rising 0.3%, including gains of 0.3% in core goods and 0.4%
in core services. Indeed, 52% of CPI categories recorded inflation above
3% y/y, with 70% above 2%.
Durable goods prices jumped 0.9% m/m in September (chart). Notably,

prices of computers, peripherals, and smart home assistants rose 1.3%,
pushing this three-month moving average inflation rate to a record high.
That reflects surging semiconductor prices driven by the AI investment

### Page 6

boom. Tariffs are also adding to inflation, with prices of other household
equipment and furnishings jumping 2.1% m/m.
Nondurable goods prices jumped 1.4% m/m in September, led by a 9.3%
surge in motor fuel prices (chart).
The services sector also showed broad-based inflationary pressures, with
prices rising 0.4% m/m (chart). Higher energy costs are passing through
to transportation and travel services, while robust aggregate demand is
sustaining pricing power.

### Page 7

(2) CPI inflation (y/y). Both headline and core inflation accelerated on a
y/y basis in September, marking the 67th consecutive month that inflation
has remained above the Fed's 2% target (chart).
Both goods and services inflation rose in September (chart). With durable
goods deflation unlikely to return soon, services inflation must fall more to
bring inflation back to target. However, services inflation remains
stubbornly elevated.

### Page 8

Supercore CPI inflation rose to 3.2% y/y in September, underscoring the
persistence of underlying price pressures (chart).
III. The Fed's Beige Book
The Fed's October Beige Book, which summarizes economic conditions
across the 12 Federal Reserve districts, reinforced the September CPI
report's message that inflation remains troublesome.
Half the districts reported robust or accelerating price pressures,
compared with broadly stable inflation in September. Four districts

reported accelerating inflation, while three characterized price growth as
robust, up from one previously. Higher energy costs, tariffs, and insurance
premiums were the main drivers, with diesel costs passing through via

### Page 9

freight surcharges (chart). Expiring hedges and contract renewals threaten
further increases.
Meanwhile, economic activity remains robust, supported by strong
manufacturing demand from AI data centers, defense, and energy.
